Finally, a causal temporal encoder learns latent risk evolution patterns and predicts the distribution of future risk emergence turns. Extensive experiments across 7 risk categories show that Recast predicts 88.3% of future safety failures with an average lead time of 2.41 turns, while maintaining a false alarm rate of 12.3%, showcasing the effectiveness of trajectory-level forecasting in identifying emerging risks before safety violations occur.

</details>


### [192] [Budget-Aware LLM Discovery via Cost-Calibrated Frontier Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26828)
*Yansen Zhang,Yilu Liu,Tianyu Liu,Jiamin Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Kai Xie,Xue Liu,Chen Ma,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CostAda，一种成本校准的自适应控制器，用于大语言模型在推理时进行搜索发现。现有方法仅根据得分进度分配信用，忽视不同搜索动作（如提示长度、重试、指导调用）的令牌成本差异。作者证明，成本盲信用在边界多样且成本发散时会导致几乎全部质量损失。CostAda 通过成本校准的边界效用，将边界进度与实现成本及剩余预算关联，从而控制局部探索强度、边界分配和预算策略干预。实验表明，CostAda 在 16 个基准-骨干对中的 12 个上，以最多一半的预算达到最强基线的全预算质量，并在所有 8 个基准上取得最强平均最终质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应发现控制器仅基于得分进度分配信用，忽略了提示长度、重试次数和指导调用等搜索动作带来的不同令牌成本。这种成本盲信用在搜索边界增多且成本差异扩大时，会导致可达到的质量急剧下降。因此，在固定搜索令牌预算下，控制器需要判断哪个边界正在改进以及其收益是否值得已花费的成本，否则预算耗尽时将无法做出最优决策。

Method: 提出 CostAda，一种成本校准的自适应控制器，其核心是成本校准的边界效用（cost-calibrated frontier utility）。该效用将边界进度与实现动作成本相关联，并根据剩余预算对信用进行条件化。CostAda 利用这一信号来控制局部探索强度、边界分配以及预算策略干预，从而使成本和剩余预算直接塑造搜索过程，而非仅作为记账变量或停止规则。

Result: CostAda 在 16 个基准-骨干对中的 12 个上，以最多一半的预算达到了最强基线的全预算质量。在 GLM-5 和 GPT-5.4 下，CostAda 在所有 8 个基准上取得了最强的平均最终质量。

Conclusion: 将成本纳入信用分配和搜索控制能够显著提升推理时搜索的效率与质量。CostAda 证明了成本校准的边界效用是一种有效的机制，能在固定预算下更优地分配搜索资源，实现更经济的高质量发现。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly support scientific and algorithmic discovery through inference-time search over evaluated candidates. Existing adaptive discovery controllers assign credit based only on score progress, even though prompt length, retries, and guidance calls cause search actions to incur different token costs. We prove that cost-blind credit can forfeit all but a vanishing fraction of attainable quality as frontiers multiply and costs diverge. Under a fixed search-side token budget, the controller must decide which frontier is improving and whether its gain justifies the realized cost before the budget is exhausted. We introduce \textbf{CostAda}, a cost-calibrated adaptive controller built around \emph{cost-calibrated frontier utility}. The utility values frontier progress relative to realized action cost and conditions that credit on the remaining budget. CostAda uses this signal to control local exploration intensity, frontier allocation, and budgeted tactic intervention. Cost and remaining budget therefore shape the search rather than serving only as accounting variables or a stopping rule. CostAda reaches the strongest baseline's full-budget quality with at most half the budget on twelve of sixteen benchmark--backbone pairs while achieving the strongest mean final quality on all eight benchmarks under GLM-5 and GPT-5.4.

</details>


### [193] [Kairos: Numerically Robust News Recommendation under Item Cold-Start via Cholesky-based LinUCB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26832)
*Finn Hertsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向区域新闻推荐的Kairos框架，通过LinUCB上下文在线学习结合Cholesky秩一更新保证数值稳定性，并集成Matryoshka表示学习加速推理，在Tagesschau API数据上实现4.85倍效率提升且几乎不损失排序精度，为数据稀缺环境下的高性能推荐系统提供了蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域市场的算法新闻个性化面临结构性项目冷启动问题：深度学习模型需要海量交互数据，但真实新闻具有短存活期（TTL<48小时）和浅文章池，导致协同过滤无法获得足够数据进行稳健建模。

Method: 提出Kairos框架，采用LinUCB上下文在线学习应对数据稀疏性；用Cholesky因子的直接秩一更新替代易出错的Sherman-Morrison求逆，保证协方差矩阵在病态数据条件下的正定性；集成Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）降低推理延迟。

Result: 基于Tagesschau API的实证评估表明，利用特征空间中的语义冗余实现了4.85倍的效率提升，且未显著牺牲排序精度。

Conclusion: Kairos为资源受限和数据稀缺环境下的高性能推荐系统提供了一个可参考的解决方案蓝图。

Abstract: Algorithmic news personalization in regional markets often fails because modern deep learning models require massive interaction data while real-world news has a short Time-to-Live (TTL < 48 h) and shallow article pools. This structural item cold-start deprives collaborative filtering of the data needed for robust modeling. This paper presents Project Kairos, a framework that bridges this data scarcity through a contextual online learning approach (LinUCB). To ensure numerical integrity for continuous operation, Kairos replaces error-prone Sherman-Morrison inversions with direct rank-1 updates of Cholesky factors. This preserves the positive definiteness of the covariance matrix even under ill-conditioned data scenarios. Simultaneously, Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) integration addresses inference latency. Empirical evaluations based on the Tagesschau API demonstrate that exploiting semantic redundancy in the feature space achieves a 4.85-fold efficiency gain without significantly compromising ranking precision. Kairos thus provides a blueprint for high-performance recommendation systems in resource- and data-constrained environments.

</details>


### [194] [Tight Generalization Bound for AdaBoost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26838)
*Mikael Møller Høgsgaard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了 AdaBoost 的泛化误差为 $\Theta\big(\tfrac{d\ln(n\gamma^{2}/d)}{n\gamma^2}+\tfrac{\ln(1/\delta)}{n}\big)$，其中 $\gamma$ 是弱学习器保证的优势，$d$ 是弱假设类的 VC 维，$n$ 是样本量，$\delta$ 是置信参数。上界是本文的贡献，匹配的下界来自先前工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 AdaBoost 提供严格的泛化误差上界，明确其依赖于弱学习器优势 $\gamma$、弱假设类 VC 维 $d$、样本量 $n$ 和置信参数 $\delta$，从而更精确地理解 AdaBoost 的泛化能力。

Method: 结合两个已知结果：(1) AdaBoost 输出的投票分类器的投票函数具有零经验 $\gamma/2$ 间隔损失；(2) 一个新的基于间隔的投票分类器泛化界。

Result: 得到了 AdaBoost 泛化误差的紧界 $\Theta\big(\tfrac{d\ln(n\gamma^{2}/d)}{n\gamma^2}+\tfrac{\ln(1/\delta)}{n}\big)$，其中上界由本文证明，下界由先前工作匹配。

Conclusion: 本文提供的上界与已知下界匹配，从而完整刻画了 AdaBoost 在给定弱学习器优势、弱假设类复杂度和样本量下的泛化误差，揭示了间隔最大化与泛化之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In this paper we show that the generalization error of AdaBoost is $Θ\big(\tfrac{d\ln(nγ^{2}/d)}{nγ^2}+\tfrac{\ln(1/δ)}{n}\big)$, where $γ$ is the advantage guaranteed by the weak learner, $d$ is the VC-dimension of the class containing the weak hypotheses, $n$ is the sample size, and $δ$ is the confidence parameter. The contribution of this paper is the upper bound; the matching lower bound follows from prior work. The upper bound proof follows by combining the known fact that AdaBoost outputs a voting classifier whose voting function has zero empirical $γ/2$-margin loss with what is, to the best of our knowledge, a new margin-based generalization bound for voting classifiers.

</details>


### [195] [Thinking Under Uncertainty: Evidence Use and Information-Seeking in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26845)
*Hua-Dong Xiong,Xinyuan Yan,Ji-An Li,Jingming Xue,Marcelo G. Mattar,Robert C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理时思考（thinking）对大型语言模型决策行为的影响，通过匹配不确定性的双臂老虎机实验，区分了思考是提升对已有证据的利用还是促进信息寻求。结果表明，思考增强了价值导向的行动，减少了不确定性无关的决策噪声，但未表现出信息寻求的探索行为。思考长度和信息不均衡条件相关，报告置信度对决策难度更敏感。总体而言，思考改善了模型基于当前证据的行动，而非转向信息寻求策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注推理时思考对模型性能的总体提升，但未区分这一提升是由于模型更有效地利用了已有证据，还是因为模型积极寻求信息以改进未来决策。本文旨在通过控制实验分离这两种机制，深入理解思考对决策过程的影响。

Method: 使用十个开放权重的语言模型，在匹配不确定性的双臂老虎机任务中分别进行思考（thinking）和非思考（non-thinking）模式下的试验。采用认知模型将价值导向的行动和不确定性无关的决策噪声从两种探索行为（UCB式偏好和Thompson式选择变异性）中分离出来。同时分析思考长度、报告置信度以及解码器参数（如温度）的影响。

Result: 思考模式下，价值导向的行动得到增强，不确定性无关的决策噪声减少，但未出现UCB式探索，也未增强Thompson式探索。信息不均衡的历史条件与更长的思考时间相关。报告置信度对决策难度更敏感，并与所选任务证据更紧密关联。解码器参数（尤其是温度）改变了决策噪声和思考长度，但未能复现跨输出的联合模式。

Conclusion: 在受控决策环境中，推理时思考改善了模型基于已有证据采取行动的能力，但未表现出向信息寻求策略的转变。思考长度和置信度模式分别与元认知控制和监控一致，但无法确立具体过程。

Abstract: Inference-time thinking improves the performance of large language models, but aggregate outcomes do not reveal whether models use available evidence more effectively or seek information that could improve future decisions. We distinguish these responses by measuring action preference, thinking length, and reported confidence under matched uncertainty. Ten open-weight models completed matched horizon-style two-armed bandit trials in thinking and non-thinking modes. A cognitive model separated value-guided action and uncertainty-independent choice noise from two behavioral signatures of exploration: a UCB-like preference for the less-known arm and Thompson-like choice variability that increases with total uncertainty. On average, thinking strengthened value-guided action and reduced uncertainty-independent choice noise, without producing UCB-like exploration or strengthening Thompson-like exploration. Outside action, the information-imbalanced history condition, which also displayed more observations than the matched balanced condition, was associated with greater thinking length. Reported confidence became more sensitive to decision difficulty and more strongly associated with chosen task evidence. We interpret these thinking-length and reported-confidence patterns as consistent with metacognitive control and metacognitive monitoring, respectively, without establishing either process. Decoder sweeps, especially temperature, altered choice noise and thinking length but did not reproduce the joint cross-output pattern. In this controlled decision setting, thinking improved how models acted on current evidence, while neither measured signature supported a shift toward a more information-seeking policy.

</details>


### [196] [TREA-Net: A Transferable Residual Epidemiological Adaptation Network for Dengue Incidence Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26854)
*Inesh Shukla,Madhurima Panja,Tanujit Chakraborty,Chittaranjan Hens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREA-Net is a transferable residual epidemiological adaptation network for multi-week dengue forecasting under limited data. It augments neural backbones with environmental SIR model projections and learns a lightweight gated residual correction from data-rich to data-scarce regions. Using only 78-104 weeks of target data, it improves forecasting across five backbones and ten transfer settings, achieving lowest MAE when integrated with TiRex, and reducing prediction-interval width by 29.6% via conformal prediction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: New surveillance systems lack historical data for training neural forecasting models, and pretrained time-series models may not capture local epidemiological dynamics. There is a need for a lightweight, portable early-warning framework for dengue forecasting under limited data.

Method: TREA-Net augments neural forecasting backbones with projections from an Environmental Time-Series Susceptible-Infected-Recovered (ETS-SIR) model, and learns a lightweight gated residual correction that transfers knowledge from data-rich to data-scarce regions. It uses a node-invariant design to accommodate different numbers of locations, requiring only two global parameters for target adaptation.

Result: Transferred knowledge from Colombia and Nicaragua to 8-week-ahead forecasting in Mexico and Malaysia using only 78 or 104 weeks of target data. TREA-Net improved the backbone in 9 out of 10 transfer settings with statistical significance. Integrated with TiRex foundation model, it achieved lowest MAE across all target datasets. Conformal prediction reduced 8-week prediction-interval width by 29.6% in Mexico while maintaining coverage.

Conclusion: TREA-Net demonstrates potential as a lightweight and portable early-warning framework for health agencies with limited surveillance data, enabling accurate multi-week dengue forecasting even when historical data is scarce.

Abstract: Accurate multi-week dengue forecasting supports timely vector-control interventions, outbreak preparedness, and healthcare resource allocation. However, newly established surveillance systems often lack the historical data needed to train reliable neural forecasting models. Although pretrained time-series models offer promising zero-shot forecasts, their cross-domain training may not capture local epidemiological dynamics. We propose TREA-Net, a Transferable Residual Epidemiological Adaptation Network for dengue forecasting under limited data. TREA-Net augments neural forecasting backbones with projections from an Environmental Time-Series Susceptible-Infected-Recovered model and learns a lightweight gated residual correction transferable from data-rich to data-scarce regions. Its node-invariant design accommodates surveillance systems with different numbers of locations, while target adaptation requires learning only two global parameters. We transfer knowledge from long-running dengue surveillance in Colombia and Nicaragua to 8-week-ahead forecasting in Mexico and Malaysia using only 78 or 104 weeks of target data. Across five neural backbones and ten transfer settings, TREA-Net improves the corresponding backbone in 9 out of 10 settings, with statistically significant gains. When integrated with TiRex, a foundation model for forecasting, it achieves the lowest mean absolute error across all target datasets. Conformal prediction further maintains empirical coverage while reducing 8-week prediction-interval width by 29.6% in Mexico. These results demonstrate TREA-Net's potential as a lightweight and portable early-warning framework for health agencies with limited surveillance data.

</details>


### [197] [Amortized Moment Matching for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26860)
*Wenze Liu,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经网络的摊销矩匹配方法（Amortized Moment Matching, AMFD），通过学习数据矩作为分布训练信号，实现高效的生成模型训练，在单步生成和指令跟随能力上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Fréchet距离（FD）损失依赖于显式的边缘矩计算，难以扩展到高维数据，且训练动态不稳定。本文旨在设计一种能够动态学习条件矩、无需显式矩阵计算的、可扩展的分布匹配损失函数。

Method: 将扩散去噪器通过多项式投影进行建模，建立矩摊销的通用框架，证明n次投影能显式识别n+1阶数据矩。从可解的仿射情况出发，推导出AMFD损失。采用交替、无矩阵的优化流程，动态学习条件矩，可直接应用于全局表示特征或原生生成空间。

Result: 在ImageNet上单步生成取得优异结果；在FDr^6指标上显著超越FD基线；在文本到图像生成中，单步模型在GenEval基准上超越多步FLUX.2 4B教师模型，在PickScore上达到同等水平。

Conclusion: AMFD提供了一种更鲁棒、可扩展的分布匹配训练目标，能够有效利用前两阶矩在强语义空间中识别目标分布，释放指令跟随能力的巨大提升。

Abstract: We propose amortized moment matching, utilizing neural networks to learn data moments as distributional training signals. By casting diffusion denoisers through polynomial projections, we establish a general framework for moment amortization, revealing that an $n$-th degree projection explicitly identifies data moments up to order $n+1$. Derived from the tractable affine case, we instantiate the Amortized Fréchet Distance (AMFD) loss. Unlike FD-loss which relies on explicit marginal moment calculations, AMFD is able to dynamically learn conditional moments via an alternating, matrix-free optimization pipeline that effortlessly scales to high-dimensional data. When operating on global representation features, AMFD serves as a powerful post-training objective; empirically, its neural formulation yields more robust training dynamics than exact statistical matching, substantially surpassing the FD baseline on the FDr$^6$ metric and achieving superior one-step generation on ImageNet. Furthermore, it unlocks direct exploration within native generative spaces, suggesting that the first two moments can identify target distributions only in spaces with strong semantics. Finally, when scaled to text-to-image generation, the condition-aware nature of AMFD unlocks massive gains in instruction-following capabilities, enabling our one-step models to outperform their multi-step FLUX.2 [klein] 4B teachers on the GenEval benchmark while achieving on-par performance on PickScore. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/poppuppy/amfd.

</details>


### [198] [ReCo: Reweighting GRPO Against Distributional Concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26862)
*Junoh Park,Junseo Hwang,Wonguk Cho,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO在训练语言模型时会降低基础模型的推理能力，表现为Pass@k在k较大时下降。本文发现这是因为GRPO的更新机制使得高概率响应主导梯度，并提出ReCo方法，通过对响应贡献进行归一化、用基于方差的比率替代重要性比率来缓解这一问题。在多个数学推理基准上，ReCo在大k下提升Pass@k，小k下与GRPO持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO虽已成为语言模型后训练的标准强化学习方法，但最近研究发现它会降低基础模型的推理能力，在Pass@k（k较大时）上表现不如基础模型，意味着推理路径的覆盖范围减小。作者希望探究这一现象的根源并提出改进方法。

Method: 本文提出ReCo方法，包含两个关键改进：1）在响应级，对响应贡献按其在该rollout组中的期望出现次数进行归一化，抑制高概率响应的过度影响；2）在token级，用基于方差的比率替代标准的重要性比率，使未饱和决策点（即替代token选择仍合理的token位置）获得更大的更新幅度。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B/7B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct五个数学推理基准上的实验表明，ReCo在k值较大时提升了Pass@k，在k值较小时与GRPO表现相当。

Conclusion: GRPO的推理能力下降源于其对高概率响应的过度集中，ReCo通过重新加权有效缓解了这一问题，在保持小k性能的同时提升了大k下的覆盖度，为强化学习后训练中平衡探索与利用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a standard reinforcement learning method for post-training language models. Recent work shows that GRPO can reduce the base model's reasoning capacity and underperform it in Pass@k when k is large, indicating reduced coverage of reasoning paths. We find that this reduction is associated with GRPO concentrating on responses that the base model already generates with high probability. We trace this concentration to two mechanisms in the GRPO update. At the response level, high-probability responses dominate the group gradient through repeated occurrence. At the token level, GRPO's importance ratio scales gradients, further reinforcing tokens that become more likely under the current policy. We propose ReCo, a reweighting method that addresses both effects. Response contributions are normalized by their expected occurrence within the rollout group, and the token-level importance ratio is replaced with a variance-based ratio that gives larger update scale to non-saturated decision points where alternative token choices remain plausible. Across Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B/7B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ReCo improves Pass@k for large values of k and is comparable to GRPO for small values of k.

</details>


### [199] [Actions Have Consequences: Detecting Outcome Performativity using Intervention Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26908)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OPAB（Outcome Performativity A/B Detection）的方法，用于检测预测结果对实际结果产生因果影响的现象（即结果表演性）。通过比较不同预测组（干预）的结果分布差异来检测表演性，并给出了样本复杂度界限，实验验证了方法的有效性，同时指出了不可区分区域的存在及其实际意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多领域（如姑息治疗、信用分配、推荐系统），预测可能会因果性地影响其预测的结果，这种现象称为结果表演性（Outcome Performativity）。目前缺乏系统的方法来检测这种表演性，因此本文旨在形式化一种检测方法，以评估预测干预是否确实改变了结果分布。

Method: 本文形式化了结果表演性A/B检测（OPAB）方法，通过比较不同预测组（干预）下的结果分布差异来检测结果表演性。如果分布差异显著，则判定存在结果表演性。作者还推导了在不同结果表演性假设类别下的样本复杂度界限，并进行了实证验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，OPAB能够在多种情况下有效检测结果表演性。同时发现了“不可区分区域”（regions of indistinguishability），即在干预数量不足时无法检测表演性的情况。这在实际应用中具有重要意义，尤其是在样本稀缺、成本高昂或获取样本不合伦理的场景下。

Conclusion: 本文通过Open Bandits数据集案例研究验证了OPAB的有效性，并指出了未来研究方向。研究结果对结果表演性的可检测性提供了实践启示，特别是在数据受限的环境中。

Abstract: In many domains such as Palliative Care, Credit Assignment and Recommender Systems, predictions may causally influence the outcomes they predict. This phenomena is known as Outcome Performativity. This paper formalises an approach for detecting Outcome Performativity using prediction intervention called Outcome Performativity A/B Detection (OPAB). OPAB enables the detection of Outcome Performativity by assessing the dissimilarity in outcome distributions produced by different predictions groups (interventions). If that dissimilarity is significant, Outcome Performativity is detected. We derive sample complexity bounds for OPAB under various Outcome Performative assumption classes which we empirically validate. Results show that detecting Outcome Performativity using OPAB is achievable in numerous cases. Results also show the presence of regions of indistinguishability which describe settings where the allotted number of interventions are insufficient for detecting Outcome Performativity. The results of which have broader practical implications for the detectability of Outcome Performativity in settings where samples are scarce, cost-prohibitive or potentially unethical to obtain. The paper concludes with a case study on the efficacy of OPAB on the Open Bandits dataset, and provides directions for future work.

</details>


### [200] [Two Calls Beat Five Agents: Evaluating Multi-Agent Pipelines Against Self-Refinement for Local Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26922)
*Ashish Prajapati,Om Mohite*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在本地7B模型（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）上部署多智能体系统Parishad（五个角色）的效果，发现其性能受通信格式和实现细节影响较大：JSON格式因错误累积导致GSM8K准确率从75%降至45%，而纯文本格式可恢复至82%；简单的两轮自精炼（V1）在GSM8K上达到86.2%且节省7.4倍token，但在HumanEval上反而破坏性能（66.5% vs 直接96.3%）；任务感知的门控重设计（V2）在HumanEval上保持95.1%准确率。结论是通信格式和实现细节比架构复杂度更关键，简单方法可匹配或超越多智能体流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线系统在多角色任务分解方面表现出色，但现有基准测试主要使用大型商业模型，缺乏在本地小型模型上的评估。本文旨在探索多智能体系统在本地7B模型上的实际效果，并比较其与简单方法的性能差异。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct本地模型，在GSM8K（500题）和HumanEval（164题）上部署Parishad（五个角色的多智能体系统），并与直接提示和两轮自精炼策略对比。实验对比了JSON和纯文本两种通信格式，并针对HumanEval设计了任务感知的门控重设计（V2）。

Result: 多智能体系统在JSON格式下GSM8K准确率从75%降至45%（错误累积），纯文本格式恢复至82%；两轮自精炼（V1）在GSM8K上达86.2%且token用量降低7.4倍，但在HumanEval上性能从96.3%降至66.5%；任务感知门控（V2）在HumanEval上保持95.1%准确率。

Conclusion: 通信格式和实现细节比架构复杂度对性能影响更大；对于本地7B模型部署，简单方法（如两轮自精炼）可以匹配甚至超越复杂的多智能体流水线。任务感知设计能缓解多智能体系统在特定任务上的性能破坏。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipeline systems break down the task among multiple roles for better reasoning, but are benchmarked mainly with large-scale commercial models. In this study, we investigate Parishad, a structured multi-agent system involving five roles, by deploying it on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, a local model, on two datasets: GSM8K (500 questions) and HumanEval (164 questions), compared with prompting directly and two-call self-refinement. The multi-agent system drops GSM8K accuracy from 75.0\% to 45.0\% with JSON data format due to the error accumulation problem. With plaintext format, the accuracy is restored to 82.0\%. A two-call self-refinement strategy (V1) can achieve 86.2\% accuracy on GSM8K, with 7.4$\times$ lower token usage. However, the same V1 implementation on HumanEval---where direct accuracy is already 96.3\%---actively destroys performance (66.5\%). A task-aware gated redesign (V2) applied to HumanEval preserves accuracy at 95.1\%. Our results demonstrate that communication format and implementation details determine outcomes more than architectural complexity, and that simpler approaches match or outperform multi-agent pipelines for local 7B model deployment. All code and data are released.

</details>


### [201] [Temporally Centered SIGReg Improves Multi-Task LeWorldModel Learning: From Analysis to Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26924)
*Chang Liu,Fei Suo,Yanzhou Jin,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yaonan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LeWorldModel中的SIGReg（各向同性高斯正则化）在单任务中有效，但在多任务训练中会导致潜在表征混叠和性能下降。通过将SIGReg应用于时间中心化残差而非边际分布，解决了这一问题，在LIBERO基准上长程任务成功率提升1.7倍，平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%，接近大规模预训练策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWorldModel的SIGReg在单任务世界模型学习中表现良好，但无法可靠地扩展到多任务训练，导致下游行为克隆性能显著下降。需要解决边际高斯化在多任务场景下压缩任务间潜在聚类分离度、引入表征混叠的问题。

Method: 提出将SIGReg应用于时间中心化残差（temporally centered residuals）而非原始潜在边际分布。这样不对聚类中心间距施加直接正则化压力，不要求完整潜在变量服从单一各向同性高斯，同时保留抗坍塌效果。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，长程任务成功率提升1.7倍，四个套件平均成功率从53.2%提升至73.6%。无需外部预训练，略优于从头训练的Diffusion Policy，接近大规模预训练策略基线。

Conclusion: 边际高斯先验与多任务潜在结构之间存在结构性不兼容，所提方法提供了一条通往稳定、可扩展的端到端多任务世界模型学习的简单途径。

Abstract: Recent work on LeWorldModel (LeWM) has shown that the Sketched Isotropic Gaussian Regularizer (SIGReg) enables stable end-to-end world-model learning from pixels by regularizing the latent marginal distribution toward an isotropic Gaussian, thereby preventing representation collapse. While effective and elegant in single-task settings, this recipe does not extend reliably to multi-task training, leading to substantially worse downstream behavior-cloning performance. In this paper, we show that marginal Gaussianization compresses the separation between task-dependent latent clusters relative to within-cluster variation. This compression introduces representation aliasing across tasks and states, and makes the learned representations highly sensitive to small visual perturbations. To address this problem, we apply SIGReg to temporally centered residuals rather than to the latent marginal distribution. This surrogate target places no direct regularization pressure on the separation among cluster centers, removes the requirement that the full latent follow a single isotropic Gaussian, and retains the anti-collapse effect of SIGReg. On the LIBERO benchmark, our method improves downstream success on the long-horizon suite by 1.7x and raises the average success rate across four suites from 53.2% to 73.6%. Without external pretraining, it slightly outperforms Diffusion Policy trained from scratch and approaches the performance of large-scale pretrained policy baselines. These results reveal a structural incompatibility between marginal Gaussian priors and multi-task latent structure, and provide a simple route toward stable and scalable end-to-end multi-task world-model learning.

</details>


### [202] [Surrogate assisted diversity estimation in neural ensemble search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26940)
*Alexandr Udeneev,Petr Babkin,Oleg Bakhteev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双目标代理引导的神经集成搜索（NES）方法，通过两个独立训练的代理模型分别评估候选架构的预测准确性和多样性潜力，在无需穷举搜索的情况下高效识别既个体强又集体多样的架构组合，在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上取得与Deep Ensembles和Random Search相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成方法依赖模型质量与多样性，但现有神经架构搜索（NAS）计算昂贵，扩展到神经集成搜索（NES）时搜索空间指数级增长，导致计算上难以处理。需要一种高效方法同时优化个体架构与集成组成。

Method: 将候选架构表示为有向无环图（DAG），训练两个独立的代理模型分别估计预测准确性和多样性潜力，结合两者估计引导NES框架高效识别既个体强又集体多样的架构。

Result: 在FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，最终集成性能与Deep Ensembles和Random Search等标准基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 双目标代理引导的NES方法能够高效地搜索出高质量且多样性的集成模型，在多个基准数据集上表现优异，为神经集成搜索提供了可行的实用方案。

Abstract: Ensembles are a standard way to improve the performance and robustness of deep neural networks, but their effectiveness crucially depends on both the quality and the diversity of individual models. Most neural architecture search (NAS) methods are computationally expensive. Extending them to neural ensemble search (NES), which requires joint optimization of individual architectures and their ensemble composition, leads to an exponential growth of the search space and makes the problem computationally intractable. To address this, we introduce a dual-objective surrogate-guided ensemble search: candidate architectures are represented as directed acyclic graphs, and two surrogate models are trained independently to estimate predictive accuracy and diversity potential. Their combined estimates guide an NES framework that efficiently identifies architectures that are both individually strong and collectively diverse. Our final ensemble achieves competitive or superior performance compared to standard baselines such as Deep Ensembles and Random Search on FashionMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100.

</details>


### [203] [Foundation Models for Face Presentation Attack Detection: A Unified Linear-Probing Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26993)
*Peter Lorenz,Anjith George,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了24种冻结的预训练视觉基础模型（包括自监督ViT、视觉-语言编码器和监督CNN）在面部呈现攻击检测（PAD）中的表现，通过线性探测协议在MCIO基准上进行实验。研究发现，冻结的表示在数据集内表现良好，但跨数据集泛化能力有限，表明预训练模型虽包含PAD相关信息，但仍需显式适应以解决域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨数据集评估中域偏移导致基于单一数据集训练的PAD模型性能下降，而大规模标注数据稀缺。因此，研究者探究通用视觉基础模型是否已经编码了PAD相关信息，从而可以通过最小量的任务特定训练（如线性探测）直接用于PAD，避免从头训练专用架构。

Method: 使用统一的线性探测协议，保持24种预训练编码器（含自监督ViT、视觉-语言编码器、监督CNN）的骨干网络冻结，仅训练轻量线性分类头，在MCIO基准（包含MSU-MFSD、CASIA-FASD、Replay-Attack、OULU-NPU四个数据集）上进行数据集内和跨数据集评估，并比较准确率-计算量权衡。

Result: 冻结的基础模型表示在数据集内可实现强PAD性能（仅需线性分类器），但跨数据集泛化不可靠。模型规模在特定家族内有帮助但非单调，受架构和预训练方式调节。InternViT-6B获得最低平均数据集内错误率，CLIP ViT-B/32在跨数据集迁移-计算权衡中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 预训练表示确实包含PAD相关信息，但仅靠线性探测无法解决域偏移问题，显式的域适应或微调仍是必要的。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection (PAD) remains challenging under cross-dataset evaluation, where domain shift degrades models trained on a single dataset. The scarcity of large-scale labeled data motivates adapting pretrained vision models rather than training task-specific architectures from scratch, raising a fundamental question: do general-purpose vision foundation models encode PAD-relevant information accessible with minimal task-specific training? To investigate, we systematically evaluate 24 frozen encoders, including self-supervised vision transformers, vision-language encoders, and supervised CNNs, using a unified linear-probing protocol on the MCIO benchmark (MSU-MFSD, CASIA-FASD, Replay-Attack, OULU-NPU). The backbone remains fixed, and only a lightweight linear head is trained to isolate the PAD information already present in the pretrained representation. %We report intra- and cross-dataset performance, along with accuracy-compute trade-offs, relative to two specialist PAD baselines. Results show that frozen foundation-model representations can support strong intra-dataset PAD performance with only a linear classifier, but this performance does not reliably transfer across datasets. Model scale is beneficial within several families, although the effect is not monotonic and is strongly mediated by architecture and pretraining. InternViT-6B achieves the lowest mean intra-dataset error, whereas CLIP ViT-B/32 offers the most favorable cross-dataset transfer-compute trade-off among the evaluated probes. These findings suggest that while pretrained representations contain PAD-relevant information, explicit adaptation remains necessary to address domain shift.

</details>


### [204] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制干预实验，揭示潜在世界模型中预测目标决定模型学习哪些物理量：输入限制可恢复性，预测目标决定保留性。刚度仅当预测触摸时进入潜在表示；阻力在确定性预测目标下无法学习；额外数据仅改善已获取参数，不获取新参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解潜在世界模型为何以及如何内化环境物理量，明确哪些物理参数被编码到潜在表示中，以及决定这一过程的因素，从而指导模型设计和数据收集策略。

Method: 在POKEWORLD环境中进行受控干预实验，该环境具有视觉相同但隐藏质量、阻力和接触刚度的物体。采用证书门控协议：首先验证每个参数是否可从原始观测恢复（可恢复性认证），然后测量该参数是否进入潜在表示。在RH20T数据集上进行输入-目标因子分析，跨两个机器人和4,258个 episodes 验证缩放曲线。

Result: 识别出两种组织机制：输入限制可知性，预测目标决定保留性。刚度仅在预测触摸时进入潜在表示（R²=0.50 vs -0.02）；单步预测下视觉潜在表示丢弃完全可见的物体状态；阻力可恢复性证书0.89但在所有确定性预测目标下饱和于0.13，而监督头可达0.45。RH20T上，缺失信息或预测压力的实验保持平坦，仅完整多模态目标能预测力，且增益随规模增长。

Conclusion: 目标结构决定潜在表示获取哪些物理参数，额外数据仅改善已获取的参数，不会使模型获取新的物理参数。这一发现对设计高效的世界模型训练目标具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>


### [205] [BayesAME: Bayesian Active Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27023)
*Paula Cordero Encinar,Taylan Cemgil,Arnaud Doucet,Virginia Aglietti,Silvia Chiappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 BayesAME，一种顺序贝叶斯框架，用于在评估大型生成模型时自动确定核心集（coreset）的大小。该方法通过建模性能为随机变量、利用历史模型信息、基于信息增益准则迭代选择样本，直到性能估计波动和不确定性低于用户定义阈值，从而在保证可靠估计的前提下自动确定核心集规模。实验表明其优于现有方法，并证实非随机核心集选择优于随机选择，且使用连续响应对数似然比传统二元分数能显著提升估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型生成模型在完整基准上耗时且计算昂贵，需要仅用子集（核心集）估计完整性能的方法。现有方法通常要求用户预先指定核心集大小，但当可靠性能估计优先于效率时，需要一种能根据这一优先级自动确定核心集大小的方法。

Method: BayesAME 采用顺序贝叶斯框架，将性能建模为随机变量，为每组共享历史模型性能的样本定义潜在能力，并通过联合先验分布编码目标模型与历史模型行为相似的信念。利用后验分布推导性能估计器、量化不确定性，并通过信息增益准则选择样本加入核心集。迭代扩充核心集直到性能估计波动和不确定性低于用户定义阈值。还提出多目标扩展以捕捉多个目标模型间的性能相关性，进一步减小核心集规模。

Result: 在多种基准上的实验表明，BayesAME 始终优于现有方法的顺序改编版本。分析回答了文献中对非随机核心集选择的质疑，证实非随机选择优于随机选择。此外，使用连续响应对数似然比传统二元分数能显著提升估计精度。

Conclusion: BayesAME 能够自动确定核心集大小，在保证可靠性能估计的前提下提升效率。非随机核心集选择具有优势，连续响应对数似然有助于提高估计准确性。该框架为大型模型评估提供了一种灵活、自动化的核心集构建方案。

Abstract: Evaluating large generative models across benchmarks is time-consuming and computationally expensive. This drives the need for methods that can estimate full benchmark performance by evaluating models on only a subset of items, known as a coreset. Current literature mostly requires the practitioner to input a coreset size. However, when reliable performance estimation takes priority over efficiency, an evaluation method should also be capable of automatically determining a coreset size that reflects this priority. We introduce BayesAME, a sequential Bayesian framework specifically targeting automatic determination of the coreset size. BayesAME models performance as a random variable by defining a latent ability for each group of items sharing the same historical model performances, with a joint prior distribution encoding the belief that the target model behaves similarly to these historical models. The posterior distribution over these abilities is used to derive performance estimators, quantify performance uncertainty, and select items to add to the coreset via an information-gain criterion. The coreset is iteratively augmented until the performance estimate fluctuation and the performance uncertainty fall below their respective user-defined thresholds. We propose a multi-target extension that captures performance correlations across multiple target models to further reduce the coreset size. Through extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that BayesAME consistently outperforms sequential adaptations of existing methods. Crucially, our comprehensive analysis addresses recent skepticism in the literature, establishing that non-random coreset selection is advantageous over random selection. Finally, we highlight that leveraging continuous response log-likelihoods over traditional binary scores significantly enhances estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [206] [TreeCCA: Canonical Correlation Analysis via Gradient-Boosted Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27027)
*James Chapman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TreeCCA，首个将梯度提升树作为 CCA 编码器端到端训练的方法，利用 Eckart-Young 损失实现与 XGBoost/LightGBM 的兼容，兼具非线性精度与原生可解释性，在多视图表格数据上优于或媲美线性 CCA 和深度 CCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型相关分析（CCA）长期依赖线性或神经网络编码器，而梯度提升树（GBT）在表格数据中占主导地位但未被用于 CCA。现有 CCA 方法或缺乏非线性能力，或缺少可解释性，或需要繁琐的架构设计。TreeCCA 旨在填补这一空白，提供一种即插即用、具有原生可解释性的非线性 CCA 方法。

Method: 提出 Eckart-Young (EY) 损失函数，为每个样本提供闭式梯度，可直接作为自定义目标函数嵌入任意标准 GBT 库（如 XGBoost、LightGBM）。通过端到端训练梯度提升树作为 CCA 编码器，TreeCCA 继承了 GBT 的可靠性：无需架构设计、超参数熟悉、默认参数表现良好。同时，每棵树的分裂都会选择单一特征，因此增益重要性可直接揭示驱动跨视图相关性的输入特征，实现原生可解释性。

Result: 在合成基准上，TreeCCA 匹配或超过 Deep CCA（Signed Power: 2.61 vs 2.43；Hermite: 2.93 vs 2.89）。在稀疏基准（零线性跨视图协方差）中，TreeCCA 在 p=50 时 Precision@S=1.00，而 PMD 无法检测到信号。在 UCI HAR 传感器融合基准上，TreeCCA 以 5 倍更低成本达到与 Deep CCA 相当的性能，且 XGBoost 增益重要性直接验证了物理驱动的假设。在五个流行多视图表格数据集上，TreeMCCA 在非线性相关提取和下游分类准确率上一致匹配或超过线性 CCA。

Conclusion: TreeCCA 是首个能够以原生可解释性实现非线性 CCA 的 GBT 方法，结合了 GBT 的即插即用可靠性和卓越性能，为表格数据的多视图分析提供了实用且可解释的工具。

Abstract: Gradient-boosted trees dominate tabular machine learning, yet canonical correlation analysis has always relied on linear or neural encoders. We propose \textbf{TreeCCA}, the first method to train gradient-boosted tree ensembles end-to-end as CCA encoders, inheriting their plug-and-play reliability: no architecture design, familiar hyperparameters, and strong performance with defaults. The technical enabler is the Eckart-Young (EY) loss, which supplies closed-form per-sample gradients that slot directly into any standard GBT library (XGBoost, LightGBM) as a custom objective.
  TreeCCA is the first CCA method to combine nonlinear accuracy with native interpretability: every tree split selects one feature, so gain importances reveal which inputs drive cross-view correlation at no extra cost. We demonstrate these properties on synthetic benchmarks, where TreeCCA matches or exceeds Deep CCA (2.61 vs.\ 2.43 on Signed Power; 2.93 vs.\ 2.89 on Hermite), and on a sparse benchmark with zero linear cross-view covariance, where TreeCCA recovers the true support with $\text{Precision@}S = 1.00$ at $p=50$ while PMD finds no signal. On the UCI HAR sensor-fusion benchmark, TreeCCA achieves comparable accuracy to Deep CCA at $5\times$ lower cost, while XGBoost gain importances directly validate a physics-motivated hypothesis about the data --- an interpretation not readily available with neural encoders. Across five popular tabular multi-view datasets, TreeMCCA consistently matches or exceeds linear CCA in both nonlinear correlation extraction and downstream classification accuracy.

</details>


### [207] [Lottery Tickets Are Not Deployment Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27031)
*Bum Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部署条件下，稀疏模型（如彩票票据）能否在不重新配置下游决策逻辑的情况下替代密集模型。实验发现，尽管稀疏模型能达到与密集模型相似的准确率，但在行为上存在差异，尤其是在固定阈值策略中会导致7%-10%的决策变化，从而产生重新验证的负担。理论表明，即使精确的逐点top-1一致也无法保证固定阈值决策不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于稀疏化、压缩和彩票票据对模型行为影响的研究结果不一，且未考虑实际部署条件（决策逻辑已固定）。因此，本文从实用角度研究生产替代问题，即一个准确率匹配的彩票票据或其他稀疏挑战者能否在不重新配置下游决策逻辑的情况下替代现有的密集模型。

Method: 作者在部署层面审计了一系列协议特定的行为面板，包括校准、OOD响应、类别级可靠性、表示和下游策略决策，并用行为兼容性距离总结除清洁准确率外的偏差。进行了大量实验，并提供了理论解释。

Result: 稀疏候选者能恢复密集参考的准确率但行为不同；在多个设置中，彩票票据的污染准确率更低；在固定阈值策略诊断中，彩票票据替代改变了7%-10%的接受-审查决策。

Conclusion: 清洁准确率认证存在局限性；兼容性与固定替代者不同，不能将决策变化唯一归因于稀疏性或将所有偏差视为有害。理论解释了即使精确的top-1一致也无法约束固定阈值决策变化。

Abstract: Reports on how sparsification, compression, and lottery tickets change model behavior have been mixed in the prior literature, with beneficial effects observed in some studies and adverse effects in others. Moreover, prior work has not considered actual deployment conditions, where decision logic is already fixed for the incumbent. To assess these mixed findings from a practical standpoint, we study the production-replacement question at the deployment level, namely whether an accuracy-matched lottery ticket or another sparse challenger can replace an incumbent dense model without reconfiguring downstream decision logic. We therefore audit a broad, protocol-specific panel of deployment-relevant behaviors spanning calibration, OOD response, class-level reliability, representations, and downstream policy decisions, and summarize clean-accuracy-excluded deviations with a behavioral-compatibility distance. Across extensive experiments, sparse candidates repeatedly recover dense-reference accuracy yet remain behaviorally different; in several study-band-matched settings, LTs also show lower corruption accuracy. In small-gap settings with fixed-threshold policy diagnostics, lottery-ticket replacement changes 7% to 10% of accept--review decisions. This churn creates precisely the burden that drop-in replacement is meant to avoid: reconfiguring and revalidating downstream decision logic. These findings establish the limits of clean-accuracy certification: Establishing compatibility with a fixed incumbent is distinct from attributing churn uniquely to sparsity or treating every measured deviation as harmful. Our theory explains the routing result: Even exact pointwise top-1 agreement cannot bound fixed-threshold decision changes, and small confidence shifts near the operating boundary can generate first-order routing churn.

</details>


### [208] [CoCaRS: Correlation Calibration-Based Redundancy Suppression for Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27054)
*Fengming Yu,Haiwei Pan,Kejia Zhang,Chunling Chen,Jian Guan,Baoying Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCaRS（基于相关性校准的冗余抑制方法）用于异构知识蒸馏，通过混淆证据估计（CEE）和强度分配控制（SAC）校准特征去相关过程，以保留判别性结构信息，并引入自适应系数调节（ACR）降低对超参数设置的敏感性，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构知识蒸馏中，教师与学生模型架构差异导致表征差异大，直接知识迁移效果有限。现有冗余抑制方法通过去相关消除特征冗余，但均匀去相关可能破坏有用结构信息，且固定系数对不同师生对和训练阶段的敏感性高。

Method: 提出CoCaRS框架，包含三个组件：1）混淆证据估计（CEE）捕捉可靠的语义关系用于相关性估计；2）强度分配控制（SAC）在去相关过程中保留判别性结构；3）自适应系数调节（ACR）根据相对损失规模动态调整冗余抑制目标的贡献，降低对系数设置的敏感性。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1K数据集上的实验表明，CoCaRS有效提升了蒸馏性能，并降低了对系数设置的敏感性。

Conclusion: CoCaRS通过校准去相关过程和自适应调节系数，更好地保留结构信息的同时抑制冗余，提升了异构知识蒸馏的效果和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) enables a compact student model to learn from a powerful teacher and has become an effective paradigm for model compression. The emergence of diverse model architectures has extended KD from homogeneous to heterogeneous settings. However, differences in architectural inductive biases between the teacher and student models often result in substantial representation discrepancies, limiting the effectiveness of direct knowledge transfer. Recently, redundancy suppression has offered a new perspective on heterogeneous KD by preserving cross-architecture invariance and reducing feature redundancy through decorrelation of teacher-student feature correlations. Nevertheless, this formulation may weaken useful structural information through uniform decorrelation, while a fixed coefficient may make the effective contribution of redundancy suppression sensitive to teacher-student pairs and training stages. To address these problems, Correlation Calibration-based Redundancy Suppression (CoCaRS) is proposed to better retain structural information while suppressing redundancy and reduce sensitivity to coefficient settings across teacher-student pairs and training stages. Specifically, CoCaRS calibrates feature decorrelation through Confusion Evidence Estimation (CEE) and Strength Allocation Control (SAC), which respectively capture reliable semantic relations for correlation estimation and preserve discriminative structure during decorrelation. Adaptive Coefficient Regulation (ACR) further regulates the contribution of the calibrated redundancy suppression objective according to its relative loss scale, reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1K validate the effectiveness of CoCaRS in improving distillation performance and reducing sensitivity to coefficient settings. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [209] [Parameter-Free Dynamic Regret for Online Convex Optimization under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27073)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HT-PAder算法，在非平稳环境下实现参数自由的通用动态遗憾，适用于重尾噪声（仅有限p阶中心矩，p∈(1,2]），并证明了匹配的下界，首次在有限方差情形下给出参数自由的最优通用动态遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳环境中，现有OCO方法在重尾噪声下无法同时实现通用动态遗憾和参数自由（即无需提前知道问题参数），这是一个开放挑战。

Method: HT-PAder算法结合了基于几何块长度池的重启AdaGrad专家与路径元算法AdaGrad-Hedge（对元损失无需矩条件），实现参数自由的动态遗憾。

Result: HT-PAder在域直径D、Lipschitz常数G、噪声水平σ和比较器路径长度P_T下，达到期望通用动态遗憾Õ(GD√(T(1+P_T/D)) + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p})，并证明了匹配的下界，表明路径长度指数最优。

Conclusion: HT-PAder是首个在重尾噪声下实现参数自由通用动态遗憾的算法，即使在有限方差特例中也填补了缺失，并通过下界证实其最优性。

Abstract: We study online convex optimization (OCO) in non-stationary environments under heavy-tailed noise, where the stochastic gradient oracle admits only a finite $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1, 2]$. While static regret is well-understood, achieving universal dynamic regret in a parameter-free manner remains an open challenge. We resolve this by proposing \textbf{HT-PAder}, a parameter-free algorithm combining restarted AdaGrad experts over a geometric pool of block lengths with a pathwise meta-algorithm, \textbf{AdaGrad-Hedge}, which requires no moment conditions on meta-losses. For a domain of diameter $D$, Lipschitz constant $G$, noise level $σ$, and comparator path length $P_T$, HT-PAder achieves an expected universal dynamic regret of \[ \widetilde O\left( GD\sqrt{T(1+P_T/D)} + σD T^{1/p}(1+P_T/D)^{(p-1)/p} \right). \] The algorithm does not require prior knowledge of any of these problem parameters. Even in the special case of finite variance ($p=2$), HT-PAder provides the first parameter-free minimax universal dynamic regret guarantee. We also prove a matching lower bound, establishing the optimality of the path-length exponent.

</details>


### [210] [Single-Beat Cuffless Blood Pressure Estimation Using Ear-PPG and ECG with a Lightweight Hybrid Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27076)
*Kindeep K. Dhatt,Tengyue Wu,Hanbang Hua,Yayun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级多模态可穿戴框架，通过单拍级ECG和PPG融合实现无袖带连续血压估计，在公开数据集上取得SBP MAE 4.02 mmHg、DBP MAE 1.79 mmHg的成绩，相比基线模型联合MAE降低28.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无袖带血压监测依赖多秒窗口的脉搏波传导时间（PTT）模型，在运动伪影和生理变异性下鲁棒性不足，且实际监测中信号间歇性损坏导致假设频繁不成立，亟需一种无需长时序上下文、单拍级别的稳健血压估计方法。

Method: 集成胸部心电（ECG）和耳夹式反射光电容积描记（PPG），各配6轴惯性测量单元提供运动上下文；提出混合学习架构：1D CNN从单个PPG搏动提取64维嵌入，与30个生理特征（含PTT统计量、心率变异性）融合，经LightGBM回归输出血压。

Result: 多阶段压力测试（n=10）和PulseDB公开数据集上，经30次独立运行，SBP MAE 4.02±0.21 mmHg，DBP MAE 1.79±0.05 mmHg，联合MAE相比基线降低28.2%。

Conclusion: 实现了无需长时序上下文的单拍级血压估计，支持计算高效的可穿戴部署，源代码已开源。

Abstract: Continuous cuffless blood pressure (BP) monitoring remains challenging due to motion artifacts, physiological variability, and the limited robustness of conventional pulse transit time (PTT) models under dynamic conditions. Many prior approaches rely on multi-second windows to stabilize estimation, an assumption that is frequently violated during real-world monitoring with intermittent signal corruption. Here, we show that discriminative BP-related information is preserved at the single-beat level and present a lightweight multi-modal wearable framework for continuous BP estimation. The system integrates synchronized chest electrocardiography (ECG) and ear-clip reflectance photoplethysmography, each co-located with a 6-axis inertial measurement unit to provide motion context. We introduce a hybrid learning architecture in which a one-dimensional convolutional neural network extracts a 64-dimensional embedding from individual PPG beats and fuses it with 30 physiology-grounded features, including PTT statistics and heart rate variability, followed by LightGBM regression. The method was evaluated using a multi-phase stress protocol ($n=10$) and the PulseDB public dataset with subject-disjoint validation. Across 30 independent runs, the model achieved mean absolute errors of $4.02 \pm 0.21$~mmHg for systolic BP and $1.79 \pm 0.05$~mmHg for diastolic BP, corresponding to a 28.2\% reduction in combined MAE relative to baseline models. By enabling beat-wise estimation without long temporal context, this framework supports computationally efficient cuffless BP monitoring suitable for wearable deployment under practical resource constraints. The source code for this work is available at https://github.com/SYMBIOX-Lab/BP-wireless.

</details>


### [211] [Equilibrium Training of Energy-Based Models with Parallel Trajectory Tempering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27077)
*Nicolas Béreux,Aurélien Decelle,Cyril Furtlehner,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于并行轨迹回火（PTT）的EBM训练算法，通过利用优化路径连续性维持平衡采样，实现高效稳定的多模态小样本训练，计算成本与持久对比散度相当，在RBM和离散表格数据上超越现有方法，使EBM的平衡最大似然训练变得实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量模型（EBM）为科学数据生成建模提供了可解释框架，但马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛混合性能差常限制其可靠性，亟需改善训练算法以处理高度多模态和数据稀缺的科学数据集。

Method: 提出并行轨迹回火（PTT）训练算法，利用优化路径连续性在整个学习过程中维持平衡采样，结合储层采样和自适应优化，计算成本与持久对比散度相当。

Result: 在受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）上持续优于现有EBM训练方法；在离散表格数据上超越最先进的深度生成模型，生成更高质量样本，对过拟合和有限数据更具鲁棒性；提供热化时间、平衡样本和准确对数似然的直接估计。

Conclusion: 该工作使EBM的平衡最大似然训练变得实用且计算高效，为科学数据生成建模提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Energy-Based Models (EBMs) provide an interpretable framework for generative modeling of scientific data, but poor Markov Chain Monte Carlo mixing often limits their reliability. We introduce a training algorithm based on Parallel Trajectory Tempering (PTT), which exploits the continuity of the optimization path to maintain equilibrium sampling throughout learning. This enables stable and fast training on highly multimodal and data-scarce scientific datasets. Combined with reservoir sampling and adaptive optimization, PTT has a computational cost comparable to Persistent Contrastive Divergence, making it a practical replacement for standard training methods. It also provides direct estimates of thermalization times, equilibrium samples from trained models, and accurate log-likelihoods at essentially no additional cost. Experiments on Restricted Boltzmann Machines show that PTT consistently outperforms existing EBM training approaches. On discrete tabular data, it also surpasses state-of-the-art deep generative models, yielding higher-quality samples and greater robustness to overfitting and limited data. Our results make equilibrium maximum-likelihood training of EBMs practical and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [212] [Scores Are Not Decisions: Cost-Aware Stopping for Tool Acquisition in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27083)
*Yicheng Feng,Yan Zhang,Yan Cheng,Wei Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种代价感知的边际决策驱动停止（CAM-DF）方法，用于LLM代理在异构成本下从工具排名前缀中确定最优工具数量，在不微调底层LLM的情况下实现成本-收益权衡，在τ-bench Retail等五个工具使用领域共1,343个任务上达到最先进性能，并在实际执行中减少37%的工具暴露同时保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理依赖多种外部服务时面临工具选择困境：获取太少工具会导致任务信息不足，获取太多则会增加成本、上下文负担和隐私暴露。现有的路由器和检索器只能对候选工具进行相关性排序，但排序本身无法决定应选择多少个工具，且现有方法未能处理异构成本下的工具获取决策问题。

Method: 将工具选择问题形式化为代价感知的边际决策驱动停止问题，在排好序的工具前缀上进行停止决策；提出CAM-DF及其轻量可解释变体CAM-DF-lite；训练目标直接基于离线状态下"现在停止"与"最佳继续"之间的差距——符号标记决策方向，大小加权每个错误对应的收益损失；理论证明了该目标与停止目标的贝叶斯一致性，以及仅凭分数规则在异构成本下的次优性。

Result: 在五个工具使用领域的1,343个任务上评估；在τ-bench Retail上达到可部署方法中最高的收益，在所有五种排名来源和两种成本制度下均优于"先预测后阈值"基线；在异构成本和高成本压力下达到最先进水平，在较弱排名下增益更大；实际执行中，CAM-DF使代理暴露于比完全访问少37%的工具，同时保持可比较的任务成功率。

Conclusion: CAM-DF系列是一种轻量级的预执行插件，能够将现有的工具排名转化为更低成本的获取决策，无需微调底层LLM；在异构成本和成本压力大的场景下表现优异，能够有效平衡工具使用的收益与成本。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly depend on diverse external services such as search engines, databases, and connectors, agent harnesses face a fundamental tool-selection challenge: acquiring too few tools leaves the task under-informed, while too many adds cost, context load, and privacy exposure. Routers and retrievers can rank candidate tools by relevance, but a ranking alone does not determine how many are worth selecting. Existing approaches leave acquisition under heterogeneous costs unaddressed. We formulate this decision as cost-aware marginal decision-focused stopping (CAM-DF) over ranked tool prefixes, with CAM-DF-lite as a compact interpretable variant. We train directly on the offline gap between stopping now and the best continuation: its sign labels the decision, its magnitude weights each error by the payoff at stake. We prove this objective is Bayes-aligned with the stopping target and that score-only rules are suboptimal under heterogeneous costs. We evaluate on 1,343 tasks across five tool-use domains. On $τ$-bench Retail, CAM-DF attains the highest payoff among deployable methods, with gains over a predict-then-threshold baseline across all five ranking sources and two cost regimes. Our approach is state-of-the-art under heterogeneous costs and high cost pressure, with larger gains under weaker rankings. In live execution, CAM-DF exposes the agent to 37\% fewer tools than full access while maintaining comparable task success. The CAM-DF family is a lightweight pre-execution plugin that turns existing tool rankings into lower-cost acquisition decisions without fine-tuning the underlying LLM.

</details>


### [213] [Sky sphere representation in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27092)
*Aleksandr Berdnikov,Yevgeny Liokumovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了约100B参数规模的语言模型是否在其残差流中包含可解码的夜空地图表征。结果表明，大多数开源模型确实存在这种表征，甚至在高维主成分中显现，并通过留一法测试验证了其有效性（R²达65-85%，中位角度误差12°-21°）。该表征是首个弯曲高维不可约特征流形的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大规模语言模型内部是否编码了具有空间几何结构的真实世界知识（如夜空星图），并验证这种表征是否超越简单的线性相关泄漏，为理解模型内部表征的复杂性和几何结构提供新视角。

Method: 对约100B参数的开源语言模型进行探针分析，从残差流中解码夜空地图表征。使用主成分分析检验表征是否显现在顶层主成分上，并通过留一法（LOO）测试评估解码准确率（R²分数和中位角度误差），同时验证表征是否源自相关平面表征的简单泄漏。

Result: 大多数开源模型在残差流中包含可解码的夜空地图表征，该表征在回答夜空相关问题时出现在主成分中。留一法测试显示除一个模型外，其余模型的R²评分达65-85%，中位角度误差为12°-21°。证实该表征并非来自相关平面表征的简单泄漏。

Conclusion: 大规模语言模型内部确实存在弯曲高维不可约特征流形（夜空地图表征），这是此类特征流形的首个实例。表明模型内部编码了具有复杂几何结构的世界知识，为可解释性研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: We analyze whether language models of size ~100B have a representation of the night sky map that is decodable from their residual stream. We find that most of the considered open-source models do have such a representation, and it often even surfaces to the top principal components on prompts that ask questions like ``what is close to this object in the night sky''. In all but one model this representation showed significant scores in LOO testing, containing up to 65-85% of variance ($R^2$-score) and having median angular error down to $12^\circ-21^\circ$. We verify that our representation is not a simple leak from a correlated flat representation. To our knowledge, this representation is the first example of a curved high-dimensional irreducible feature manifold.
  Codes used in the paper are published at https://github.com/l3erdnik/Decodable-sky

</details>


### [214] [Hierarchical Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Coherent Emergency Department Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27106)
*Filipa Lino,Bárbara Tavares,Carlos Santiago,Cláudia Soares,Manuel Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HierSTT，一种基于 Transformer 的分层预测框架，能够在医院、地区、国家三个层面联合且一致地预测急诊科（ED）需求，并在葡萄牙全国数据集上相比非分层深度学习基线降低 32% 的加权绝对百分比误差（WAPE），同时优于经典分层协调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有急诊科需求预测大多在单一层级独立建模，忽略了医院、地区、国家之间的层次关联，导致各层级预测结果不一致（例如医院层预测总和与地区/国家层预测不匹配），无法有效支持多级决策（医院排班、地区协调、国家容量规划）。

Method: 提出 HierSTT，一个端到端的分层 Transformer 框架：使用 Temporal Fusion Transformer 捕获国家级动态，使用时空 Transformer 编码器-解码器模块建模地区级和医院级需求（以高层级预测为条件）；引入一致性感知损失函数惩罚跨层级不一致性。

Result: 在包含 81 家医院、5 个地区卫生管理局的葡萄牙全国 ED 数据集上，HierSTT 相比最佳非分层深度学习基线降低 32% 的 WAPE，且优于所有经典分层协调方法，同时输出近乎一致的跨层级预测。

Conclusion: 层次化端到端建模能有效提升多级急诊需求预测的准确性和一致性，HierSTT 为医疗系统的多级规划提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Emergency Departments (EDs) are critical access points in healthcare systems, yet they face persistent pressure from unpredictable patient demand, seasonal surges, and non-urgent visits. Effective ED planning requires forecasts at multiple decision-making levels: hospitals need local demand estimates for staffing and bed management, regions require forecasts to coordinate healthcare units, and national authorities need system-wide projections for capacity planning. However, most existing approaches forecast ED demand independently at a single level, ignoring the hierarchy linking hospitals, regions, and national systems. This can produce incoherent predictions, where hospital-level forecasts do not aggregate consistently to regional or national demand. We propose HierSTT, a hierarchical Transformer-based framework for coherent multi-level ED forecasting. HierSTT jointly predicts hospital, regional, and national level demand in a single end-to-end model. A Temporal Fusion Transformer captures national dynamics, while spatio-temporal Transformer encoder-decoder modules model regional and hospital demand conditioned on higher-level forecasts. A coherence-aware loss penalizes cross-level inconsistencies during training. We further introduce a nationwide Portuguese ED dataset covering 81 hospitals across 5 regional health administrations, with heterogeneous covariates at each level. Experiments show that HierSTT reduces average WAPE by 32\% relative to the best non-hierarchical deep learning baseline and outperforms all classical hierarchical reconciliation methods, while producing near-coherent predictions across levels. Additional resources associated with this work are available at https://github.com/FilipaLino/HierSTT.

</details>


### [215] [Voronoi Histograms for Adaptive Vectorization of Expected Persistence Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27126)
*Kaifeng Zhang,Kai Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Voronoi直方图的期望持续图（EPD）向量化方法，替代传统的高斯或景观函数变换，通过自适应分区计数实现高效拓扑特征表示，并建立了稳定性界限，在分类与降维任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续图（PD）能有效捕获点云拓扑，但计算复杂度高；期望持续图（EPD）通过研究多个子集降低时间成本，但现有EPD向量化方法（如高斯变换、景观函数）依赖预定义的点变换，计算开销仍较大。因此，需要一种更高效、自适应的EPD向量化方法。

Method: 提出基于Voronoi图的直方图作为EPD的向量化方法，用自适应分区计数替代平滑函数逼近。在分离和归一化条件下，推导了该表示的稳定性界限，并刻画了其保持Wasserstein尺度变化的条件。

Result: 在具有显著拓扑特征的真实数据集上，该方法在分类和降维任务中表现有效，验证了Voronoi直方图作为EPD向量化的实用性和稳定性。

Conclusion: Voronoi直方图是一种有效的EPD离散化方案，在保持拓扑信息的同时降低了计算复杂度，为持续图向量化提供了新的自适应分区思路。

Abstract: Persistence Diagram (PD) is known to capture point cloud topology effectively, but its computation has high time complexity. Expected Persistence Diagram (EPD) has been developed to reduce the time cost by studying the topology of multiple subsets of a point cloud and it serves as a distribution of topological features. Existing EPD vectorizations often rely on predefined point transformations, such as Gaussian or landscape functions. We study an alternative discretization based on Voronoi histograms, which trades smooth functional approximation for adaptive partition-based counting. We propose to use Voronoi Diagram-based histogram as the vectorization of EPD, without imposing an explicit smooth point transformation model. Under stated separation and normalization conditions, we establish stability bounds and characterize when the histogram representation preserves Wasserstein-scale variation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed representation on real-world datasets which have significant topological features for classification and dimensionality reduction tasks.

</details>


### [216] [Minimal Markovization via Stable Quotients in Holonomy-Cover Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27132)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Yongshan Chen,Mahdi Imani,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中最小马尔可夫充分统计量的表征问题，针对一类结构化的POMDP——holonomy覆盖决策过程，构建了稳定商（stable quotient）作为最粗的观测层面抽象，证明了当前观测与稳定类的配对构成精确的有限马尔可夫状态，并提出了Holonomy Memory强化学习框架，实验表明该方法能以极少的记忆状态实现精确压缩和完美决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，智能体需要维护一个可递归更新的历史统计量以恢复马尔可夫性质，但最小这样的统计量通常是未知的。本文旨在针对holonomy覆盖决策过程这一结构化POMDP类，刻画其最小马尔可夫充分统计量。

Method: 首先构建稳定商（stable quotient），即保持一步奖励和商后继的最粗观测层面抽象，并证明当前观测与稳定类的配对构成精确的有限马尔可夫状态。然后分析了在正确初始化下精确类跟踪所需的最小记忆符号数，并给出了可重置诊断下的近邻原型类推断方法及校准-重启归约。最后提出了Holonomy Memory强化学习框架，通过有序边传输更新记忆，利用诊断信息识别局部类坐标，并在同步后应用标准有限MDP强化学习算法。

Result: 实验表明该方法能从原始状态恢复精确压缩到商状态，仅用三个决策时刻记忆状态即可实现完美配对顺序精度，匹配商先知（quotient oracle）的性能，并优于非先知基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文的理论结果为holonomy覆盖决策过程提供了最小马尔可夫充分统计量的构造性刻画，并在此基础上开发的Holonomy Memory强化学习框架能在部分可观测环境中高效学习，具有重要的理论价值和实践潜力。

Abstract: An agent acting under partial observability must retain a recursively updateable statistic of history that restores the Markov property, but the smallest such statistic is generally unknown. We characterize this minimal Markov sufficient statistic for holonomy-cover decision processes, a structured POMDP class in which the visible dynamics are Markov and every realized visible transition applies a fixed permutation to a hidden mode. In particular, we construct the stable quotient, the coarsest observation-wise abstraction preserving one-step rewards and quotient successors, and prove that the pair of the current observation and stable class forms an exact finite Markov state. When the current class is correctly initialized, exact class tracking requires exactly the minimal memory symbols, in the sense that under reachability and pairwise decision separation at a maximizing observation, no arbitrary finite-memory controller can use fewer. Under resettable diagnostics, nearest-prototype class inference has exponentially decaying error, and a calibrate-then-restart reduction transfers finite-MDP guarantees to the recovered state. The results enable \emph{Holonomy Memory Reinforcement Learning}. It represents memory by the current stable class, updates it through ordered edge transports, identifies local class coordinates when diagnostics are available, and applies a standard finite-MDP RL backbone after synchronization. Experiments recover an exact compression from raw states to quotient states and achieve perfect paired-order accuracy with three decision-time memory states, matching the quotient oracle and outperforming the non-oracle baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Cost-Sensitive Conformal Prediction and Human-in-the-Loop Abstention for Imbalanced High-Stakes Decision Support: A Multi-Domain Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27143)
*Manpreet Singh,Akshatha Srikantha,Shyamal Lakhanpal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了边际共形预测（CP）、类别条件（Mondrian）CP和成本控制弃权机制在不平衡数据上的表现，发现Mondrian CP能将少数类覆盖率平均提升61.7个百分点，且结合成本控制弃权可显著降低决策成本，为高风险决策系统提供了实用的不确定性量化指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险决策系统（如信用评分、欺诈检测、医疗、工业安全）需要在严重类别不平衡和非对称错误成本下进行可靠的不确定性量化，但标准边际共形预测对稀有、高成本的少数类覆盖严重不足（低至0.5%），亟需改进方法。

Method: 在15个真实不平衡表格数据集、7个分类模型、3种概率校准技术和10个随机种子上（共3150次实验），基准比较了边际CP、Mondrian（类别条件）CP和成本控制弃权机制。

Result: Mondrian CP恢复了少数类的有效覆盖，平均少数类覆盖率比边际CP提升61.7个百分点（p<1e-80）；Mondrian CP与成本控制弃权结合，在现实人工审核预算下显著降低了期望决策成本，并量化了数据集特定的盈亏平衡阈值。

Conclusion: 研究表明Mondrian CP结合成本控制弃权是高风险不平衡分类任务中有效的分布无关、成本感知的不确定性量化方案，为实际部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: High-stakes decision systems in credit scoring, fraud detection, healthcare, and industrial safety require reliable uncertainty quantification under severe class imbalance and asymmetric error costs. Standard marginal conformal prediction (CP) provides valid overall coverage guarantees; however, we show that it severely under-covers rare, costly minority classes, with minority-class coverage dropping to as low as 0.5% on certain datasets. To characterize and address this limitation, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark comparing marginal CP, class-conditional (Mondrian) CP, and cost-controlled abstention mechanisms across 15 real-world imbalanced tabular datasets, 7 classification models, 3 probability calibration techniques, and 10 random seeds, resulting in 3,150 experimental runs. Our results show that Mondrian CP restores valid minority-class coverage, achieving an average minority-coverage improvement of 61.7 percentage points over marginal CP (p < 1e-80). Furthermore, combining Mondrian CP with cost-controlled abstention significantly reduces expected decision cost compared with standard decision boundaries, confidence-based rejectors, and risk-controlled rejectors under realistic human review budgets. We further quantify dataset-specific break-even thresholds at which deferring ambiguous instances to human experts becomes cost-effective. These findings provide practical guidance for deploying distribution-free, cost-aware uncertainty quantification in high-stakes decision support systems.

</details>


### [218] [Skillful forecasting of offshore winds from satellite scatterometer constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27152)
*Francesco Pinto,Luca Lanzilao,Paco Lopez Dekker,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WindCastNet 是首个基于卫星散射计观测数据的离岸风速和风向即时预报框架，采用部分卷积长短期记忆网络处理非规则时空采样数据，在北海区域验证中相较于数值天气预报模型 HARMONIE MEPS 在1小时和2小时预见期分别降低均方根误差 23% 和 7%，为短期离岸风电预报提供了独立且具有竞争力的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离岸风电的日内精准预报对电力系统运行和高比例风电并网日益关键。现有业务预报依赖数值天气预报（NWP），但其针对分钟至小时级预见期（此时初始条件精度主导预报技巧）并未优化。尽管卫星散射计观测已常规同化于 NWP，但从未被直接用于预报。因此，亟需一种直接利用卫星观测的即时预报方法。

Method: 提出 WindCastNet，一种部分卷积长短期记忆（Partial Convolutional LSTM）网络。该网络利用欧洲、中国和印度散射计星座的微波雷达观测，克服其空间覆盖不规则、采样异步、重访时间多变的问题。通过编码空间观测掩码和观测间隔，并采用连续时间表示，实现任意预见期的离岸风场预报。

Result: 在北海区域的评估中，WindCastNet 在1小时和2小时预见期相对于 HARMONIE MEPS 模型分别降低均方根误差 23% 和 7%；在前三小时预报中优于持续性预报 9%~15%。强风条件和非均匀空间流场下预报技巧降低。结果表明卫星散射计星座可提供独立且有竞争力的短期离岸风预报。

Conclusion: 卫星散射计星座能够为短期离岸风电预报提供独立且具竞争力的来源，开创了可再生能源预报的新机遇，并可推广至更广泛的海洋天气应用（如热带气旋即时预报）。

Abstract: Accurate intraday forecasts of offshore wind are becoming increasingly important for power system operation and the integration of growing shares of offshore wind energy. Operational forecasts rely predominantly on numerical weather prediction (NWP), which is not optimized for lead times of minutes to hours, where initial-condition accuracy dominates forecast skill. Although satellite scatterometer observations are routinely assimilated into NWP, they have not previously been used directly for forecasting. Here we present WindCastNet, the first satellite-based nowcasting framework for offshore wind speed and direction, introducing a new paradigm for intraday forecasting that learns from spatiotemporally irregular satellite observations. WindCastNet predicts offshore wind fields from observations acquired by satellite scatterometer constellations. WindCastNet employs a partial convolutional long short-term memory network that exploits microwave radar observations from the European, Chinese, and Indian scatterometers despite their irregular spatial coverage, asynchronous sampling, and variable revisit times. Spatial observation masks and inter-observation intervals are encoded, while a continuous temporal representation enables forecasts at arbitrary lead times. Evaluated over the North Sea, WindCastNet reduces the root-mean-square error by 23% and 7% relative to the HARMONIE MEPS model at lead times of 1 and 2 h, respectively, and outperforms persistence by 9-15% during the first three forecast hours. Forecast skill decreases under strong-wind conditions and spatially non-uniform flow. These results demonstrate that satellite scatterometer constellations can provide an independent and competitive source of short-term offshore wind forecasts, opening new opportunities for renewable energy forecasting but also broader marine weather applications, including tropical cyclone nowcasting.

</details>


### [219] [When Do Learned Diffusion Proposals Help Constraint Solving? A Controlled Study on Continuous Algebraic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27169)
*Quang Bui,Sparsh Roy,Akash Gundimeda,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于学习的扩散式提议（MARC）与随机多次启动（random multi-start）在求解连续代数约束系统时的表现。结果显示，学习型提议仅在变量高维解耦的合成场景中微弱优于随机搜索，而在变量耦合或真实世界问题中优势消失；经典多次启动即可解决所有八类实际系统。论文推导了随机搜索的成功概率公式，并指出了学习型提议适用的有限区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解器在值分配上表现良好，但在结构增广上依赖枚举。扩散式提议（如MARC）被提出用于同时处理两者，但评估时常忽略随机多次启动这一强基线。本研究旨在厘清学习型提议相比随机搜索究竟带来多少增益，并刻画其有效区间。

Method: 将代数约束系统转化为因子图，使用图神经扩散去噪器提议赋值，然后在精确计算机代数能量上进行梯度下降（抛光），最后用符号检查器验证解。对比同等抛光预算下的随机多次启动，并推导其成功率公式 P=1-(1-q(n))^K，其中单次可达概率 q(n) 通过实测确定、不含自由参数。在合成簇（低维、高维、耦合变量）及八个真实系统（机器人、定位、优化、代数）上评估。

Result: 在平衡非线性菜单精度上，候选条件修复排序器（0.997）远优于随机基线（0.236）。但扩散提议相比随机多次启动仅在变量解耦的高维合成问题中微弱胜出（p<1e-70, 0.982±0.006）；一旦变量耦合，优势消失。在全部八个真实世界系统中，经典多次启动均成功求解，无一落在学习型提议的有利区间。成功率公式与实验数据吻合（MAE 0.012）。

Conclusion: 学习型提议对求解代数约束系统的提升有限，其有利区间为变量高维解耦的合成场景，在真实问题中不常见。研究强调了随机多次启动作为基线的重要性，并提供了选择求解策略的决策边界。

Abstract: Solving a continuous algebraic constraint system requires two decisions: which values satisfy the constraints, and which structural augmentation renders an unsolvable system solvable. Classical solvers answer the first well and the second only by enumeration. On that discrete decision, a candidate-conditioned repair ranker choosing among K augmentations reaches the exhaustive-search ceiling at a fraction of the calls, outperforming random (0.997 vs 0.236 balanced nonlinear menu accuracy; p < 10^-70; 0.982 +/- 0.006 across seeds) and beating a budget-matched per-candidate probe on accuracy and cost. MARC turns such a system into a factor graph, over which a graph-neural diffusion denoiser proposes assignments, descent on an exact computer-algebra energy polishes them, and an exact symbolic checker certifies solutions. Evaluations of diffusion-based proposals rarely include one control: random multi-start under the same refinement budget. Applied to our system, it sharply curtails what the learned proposal contributes on the value decision. Does it beat random multi-start at choosing satisfying assignments? Only narrowly, in a predictable regime. Across trapped low-dimensional families it ties with random restart, but dominates in high dimension, where random search fails. Once variables couple, the advantage is gone. Since all methods share one polish and one checker, best-of-K random multi-start succeeds with probability exactly 1 - (1 - q(n))^K, where q(n) is single-start reachability; one measured constant, with no free parameters, reproduces the entire curve (mean absolute error 0.012). The favorable regime is not specific to our synthetic families: across eight real-world systems in robotics, positioning, optimization, and algebra, classical multi-start solved all eight, none in the learning-favorable regime. We map the regimes in which learned proposals improve solvers.

</details>


### [220] [Inverse Learning of Latent Risk-Neutral Densities from Irregular Option Quotes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27188)
*Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk,Aadi Dash,Nicholas A. Welsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了期权价格与潜在风险中性密度恢复之间的差异。通过合成基准和NIFTY基准评估，发现双组分对数正态混合模型在整体误差上表现最佳，但深度学习算子（如DeepONet、报价变换器）在特定指标上有优势。数值条件分析解释了排名差异的原因，并表明不存在通用最优方法，而是依赖于目标的归纳偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献通常假设准确的期权价格意味着准确的风险中性密度恢复，但这一假设未必成立。作者旨在明确区分期权定价精度与密度恢复精度，并通过系统性基准测试探究不同模型的相对表现。

Method: 构建两个互补基准：一个受控合成基准（暴露模拟器真实密度进行潜在评估）和一个时序NIFTY基准（仅测试留出市场价格）。采用多种方法（双组分对数正态混合、DeepONet、报价变换器、SVI等）进行密度恢复，并通过数值条件分析（强制执行质量和正向约束后，126个定价方向中95个数值为零）解释排名差异。

Result: 双组分对数正态混合在合成基准上具有最低的聚合价格误差、L1误差、Wasserstein距离和固定尾部误差。DeepONet将1%分位数误差和方差误差分别降低39.0%和34.6%，报价变换器在结构错误指定的Merton族上将L1误差降低16.4%。在NIFTY留出数据上，验证选择的测试时自适应将DeepONet RMSE降低28.3%，但按到期日划分的混合和SVI拟合仍更为准确。

Conclusion: 不存在通用最优的密度恢复方法；最佳选择依赖于具体目标（目标依赖性归纳偏差）。数值条件分析表明，不同模型在价格空间中的有效方向差异很大，这解释了为何定价精度与密度恢复精度可能不一致。

Abstract: Accurate option prices do not imply accurate recovery of the latent risk-neutral density. We study this distinction with two complementary benchmarks. A controlled benchmark exposes simulator-truth densities for latent evaluation, while a chronological NIFTY benchmark tests only held-out market prices. A two-component lognormal mixture has the lowest aggregate price, $L^1$, Wasserstein, and fixed-tail errors on the synthetic benchmark. Learned operators retain narrower strengths: DeepONet reduces 1% quantile and variance error by 39.0% and 34.6% relative to the mixture, and a quote transformer reduces $L^1$ by 16.4% on the structurally misspecified Merton family. A numerical conditioning analysis explains why these rankings can differ: after enforcing mass and forward constraints, 95 of 126 pricing directions are numerically null, and two densities separated by $L^1 = 0.061$ produce identical prices on the covered strikes. On 524 held-out NIFTY calls, validation-selected test-time adaptation reduces DeepONet RMSE by 28.3%, but per-expiry mixture and SVI fits remain much more accurate. The evidence supports target-dependent inductive bias, not a universal winner.

</details>


### [221] [From Classification to Regression: Using a Fruitfly to Solve Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27196)
*Shady E. Ahmed,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种受果蝇感知机制启发的回归方法，使用分类思想，用有限代表性局部模式库替代复杂全局代理模型，通过相似度加权重构进行预测，应用于非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习，实现计算和内存需求的降低以及精度-存储-推理成本的显式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受果蝇感知环境机制的启发，科学数据通常占据有限且重复的输入空间区域，希望用局部模式替代复杂全局模型，以降低计算和内存开销，并在精度、存储和推理成本之间取得可调平衡。

Method: 构建一个通用框架：用有限库中的代表性局部模式替代全局代理模型；通过测量查询与存储模式之间的相似度，加权重建对应的响应值。对于动力系统，采用离线-在线工作流：离线阶段从数据或控制方程中提取模式，在线阶段仅需相似度评估和响应聚合。

Result: 该方法降低了计算和内存需求，同时提供对精度、存储和推理成本之间权衡的显式控制。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新颖高效的回归方法，适用于非线性动力系统、数据驱动回归和物理信息学习等任务，具有良好的可扩展性和可控性。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to regression tasks using classification which is motivated by the mechanism used by fruitflies to sense their environment. Specifically, we formulate a general framework for learning nonlinear input-output relationships by replacing complex global surrogate models with a finite library of representative local patterns. Since scientific data often occupy limited and recurring regions of the input space, we generate predictions by measuring similarities between a query and stored patterns, then combining their associated responses through weighted reconstruction. We apply this approach to nonlinear dynamical systems, data-driven regression, and physics-informed learning using suitable embeddings and similarity measures. For dynamical systems, our offline-online workflow extracts patterns from data or governing equations during the offline phase, while online prediction requires only similarity evaluation and response aggregation. This structure helps us reduce computational and memory demands while providing explicit control over the trade-off among accuracy, storage, and inference cost.

</details>


### [222] [Do You Really Need to Pretrain Q-Functions for Online RL Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27203)
*Perry Dong,Ron Polonsky,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Fin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在基于价值的强化学习中，预训练策略后进行微调是常见做法，但本文发现直接预训练Q函数通常益处甚微，因为预训练阶段学习的Q函数与在线微调收敛的目标Q函数存在根本性不匹配。为此提出基于策略集成的初始化方法（IPE），通过训练多个多样策略并利用其混合轨迹来引导在线RL中的Q函数学习，在连续控制基准上平均提升1.26倍微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为预训练Q函数有助于微调，但近期研究表明在线RL中使用随机初始化的Q函数也能获得高性能策略。本文旨在系统研究在预训练基础策略之上进行微调时，预训练Q函数是否真正有益。

Method: 首先通过实验揭示预训练Q函数与在线微调目标Q函数之间的不匹配问题。然后提出IPE方法：离线阶段训练多个多样化的策略，利用这些策略的混合轨迹来初始化Q函数，从而为在线RL提供更好的起点。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准任务上，IPE相比朴素Q函数预训练平均获得1.26倍的微调性能提升。

Conclusion: 预训练Q函数未必有利于微调，而IPE通过策略集成初始化能更有效地桥接预训练与微调之间的目标差距，提升最终性能。

Abstract: Pre-training followed by fine-tuning has become the dominant recipe for learning performant policies, and in value-based reinforcement learning (RL) this raises a natural question: given a pretrained policy, should the Q-function be pretrained on offline data too? Conventional wisdom suggests it should, but recent results show that online RL with a randomly-initialized Q-function can result in highly performant and reliable policies without needing to pretrain the Q-function. In this paper, we systematically study whether pretraining the Q-function actually helps when fine-tuning on top of a pretrained base policy. We find, surprisingly, that naive Q-function pretraining often provides little benefit over random initialization. We show this stems from a fundamental mismatch: the Q-function learned during pretraining targets the pretrained policy's Q-function, not the Q-function that online fine-tuning converges to, and this gap persists even after offline value maximization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Initialization via Policy Ensemble (IPE), a simple method that trains multiple diverse policies and uses their pooled rollouts to bootstrap the Q-function learning in online RL. Across a suite of challenging continuous control benchmarks, IPE yields an average 1.26x improvement in fine-tuning performance over naive Q-function pre-training.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119)
*Antyabha Rahman,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Omar Ankit,Kevin Zhu,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过线性探针和均值消融实验，揭示了强化学习（RL）训练的大规模推理模型在数学推理任务上优于监督微调（SFT）模型的内在机制：RL模型形成更线性可分的层次化表征，深层网络逐渐变得关键，而SFT模型则均匀分布重要性。此外，RL模型在token分配变异性上表现不一，取决于整体训练流程而非单纯RL与SFT的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习（RL）训练的大规模推理模型为何在数学推理任务上显著优于监督微调（SFT）模型，从内部表征差异的机制层面解释这一优势。

Method: 采用两种互补的分析方法：（1）线性探针（linear probes）训练于逐层隐藏状态，比较RL与SFT模型预测答案正确性的准确率；（2）均值消融（mean ablation）研究各层对模型输出的重要性分布。此外，通过重复采样分析token分配变异性以评估自适应计算分配。

Result: RL模型相比SFT模型，隐藏状态中预测答案正确性的线性探针准确率更高，表明表征更线性可分离且结构化；均值消融显示RL模型形成层次化架构，深层逐渐更关键，而SFT模型各层重要性均匀。token分配变异性方面，部分RL模型比SFT变异性高，但另一些高度一致，表明其更多取决于整体训练流程而非RL与SFT本身。

Conclusion: RL训练从根本上重构了模型处理推理问题的表征与计算方式，其优势源于更结构化、层次化的内部表征。token分配变异性揭示了合理的在线推理策略的扩散程度，反映了模型策略的稳定性与不确定性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. We therefore ask, what internal representational differences enable RL models' superior performance? Our work presents two converging lines of evidence: First, linear probes trained on layer-wise hidden states reveal that RL models tend to achieve higher accuracy in predicting answer correctness compared to SFT models, indicating more linearly separable and structured representations. Second, mean ablation studies show that RL models develop a hierarchical architecture where deeper layers become progressively more critical, whereas SFT models distribute importance uniformly across layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that RL training fundamentally restructures how models represent and process reasoning problems. Finally, we analyze token-count variability under repeated sampling across problems to assess adaptive compute allocation. While we observe higher variability in some RL-tuned models than in their SFT counterparts, we see strong consistency in others, suggesting that token allocation may depend more on the overall training pipeline than on RL versus SFT alone. We believe this token-allocation variability reveals the spread of plausible on-policy reasoning, highlighting which models exhibit stable policies versus those that are under-determined, potentially non-identifiable solution behaviour.

</details>


### [224] [Even More Deception: Objective Misalignment in Mixed-Motive LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26120)
*Marylou Fauchard,Florian Carichon,Margarida Carvalho,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用社交推理游戏《狼人杀》来评估LLM驱动的多智能体系统中目标错位（objective misalignment）的框架。通过修改单个智能体的目标同时保留其角色，分析了来自4个模型家族、4种角色和3种目标设定下的内部推理和公开交流行为。结果表明，目标错位会损害集体决策，且这种影响在非对称信息和专业角色下加剧；受损智能体会发展出依赖目标的推理策略，但这些策略在公开行为中几乎不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统越来越多地部署在混合动机环境中，智能体在不对称信息和战略欺骗下运作，与集体目标的对齐成为一个核心问题。现有方法缺乏对目标错位的系统评估框架，因此需要一种方法来检测和量化这种错位及其影响。

Method: 使用《狼人杀》作为测试平台，修改单个智能体的目标（使其与集体目标错位）同时保留其角色。跨4个LLM模型家族、4种玩家角色和3种目标公式化设定，进行双重分析：一方面分析智能体的内部推理（思维链），另一方面分析其公开的廉价谈话（cheap-talk）行为，并结合游戏结果分析。

Result: 目标错位会显著破坏原本对抗性环境中的游戏结果，这种效应在非对称信息和专业角色（如狼人、预言家）下更为严重。受损智能体在内部推理中发展出清晰的目标依赖推理策略，但这些策略在公开交流行为中几乎不可见，暗示目标错位难以通过表面行为检测。

Conclusion: 即使是微妙的目标错位也会深刻影响多智能体系统的集体决策，突显了为LLM驱动的多智能体系统开发有效缓解策略的必要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs)-powered multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed in mixed-motive environments, where agents operate under asymmetric information and strategic deception due to conflicting or hidden objectives. In these settings, misalignment with collective goals becomes a central concern. We propose a novel framework for evaluating objective misalignment using the social deduction game Werewolf, modifying the objective of a single agent while preserving its assigned role. Across LLMs from four different model families and sizes, four player roles, and three objective formulations, we introduce a dual analysis of the agents' internal reasoning and their public cheap-talk behavior (i.e costless, non-binding communication that does not directly affect the agents' utilities), complemented by an analysis of game outcomes. Our results show that objective misalignment undermines outcomes in inherently adversarial environments, an effect exacerbated by asymmetric information and specialized roles.
